Rustani - Third-party notices for the WebAssembly distribution

Scope: 126 external Cargo packages in the resolved normal dependency graph for
package gridcraft-web targeting wasm32-unknown-unknown, excluding procedural
macro packages and dependencies reached only through those macros. Build, dev
and native-target-only dependencies are outside this inventory. Resolution was
performed offline with the existing Cargo.lock; no compilation was performed.
This is a resolved dependency inventory, not a per-symbol linker reachability
analysis. Rust toolchain/standard-library components are outside Cargo metadata.

The inventory also includes the separate licenses of four default egui fonts
and the embedded BIZ UDPGothic font. Application MIT/Apache licensing does not
replace the licenses of these components or fonts. AND requirements, including
encoding_rs BSD-3-Clause and unicode-ident Unicode-3.0, are retained. For
self_cell, the Apache-2.0 alternative is selected; GPL-2.0-only is not selected.
Legacy slash-separated MIT/Apache declarations are shown as declared, with both
original license texts reproduced.

Each package below lists its name, version, public source and declared license.
It refers to one or more TEXT identifiers. Identical original files are included
once under the same identifier; every referenced original text appears in full
later in this document, without alterations. Copyright-holder email addresses
inside original license text are preserved. Extra font copyright lines are
transcribed from the embedded font metadata where the generic license text
itself does not name the copyright holder.

License originals omitted from nine crates.io package archives were obtained
from their official upstream repositories at the exact .cargo_vcs_info.json
commit: AccessKit/accesskit a55d3e1a18bb9ef0e4bccc9083fb13c3e0ad8969;
emilk/egui 49682f8baa058bf49e011035cfbd6e825f88a5ef;
aclysma/profiling 8271551172eb6fa4cba47369aedd93790c623df9.
The AccessKit Chromium notice and craft-ui workspace NOTICE are preserved.

Original file: NOTICE
Public source: https://github.com/storytold/craft-libs/tree/d27646a5f7c8b4fc85d9dd3213b8c88b58e0088d

craft-libs
Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the craft-libs contributors.

(LICENSE-MIT, LICENSE-APACHE).

Third-party material

This product bundles third-party material under its own licenses, listed below. The per-file
list (author, source, license) is in ATTRIBUTION.md.

Source provenance

The SVG icon preparation in crates/ui is adapted from:
- PhotoCraft, crates/ui-egui/src/icons.rs, revision e7a83ca342cdff568254f85f442b9cc8afb08066.
  https://github.com/storytold/photocraft
  Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the PhotoCraft contributors.
- VectorCraft, crates/ui-egui/src/icons.rs, revision 825d4fdb8b527c597855d273fb4bcc1971855b6a.
  https://github.com/storytold/vectorcraft
  Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the VectorCraft contributors.
The icon-button interaction/style boundary is adapted from PhotoCraft (above) and:
- PdfCraft, crates/ui-egui/src/icons.rs, revision f626db25cca2c89f8fa3f7e30d22bf59faea65ec.
  https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft
  Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the PdfCraft contributors.
The tab fitting in crates/ui/src/tabs is adapted from PhotoCraft's tab_strip.rs (above).
Tab interaction adapters also use VectorCraft's dock.rs and floating.rs (above).
The pane sizing and resize boundaries in crates/ui/src/layout draw on:
- FilmCraft, crates/ui-egui/src/dock.rs, revision 7b6c134472287db4367fca80bcb498240ba36567.
  https://github.com/storytold/filmcraft
  Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the FilmCraft contributors.
- EffectCraft, crates/ui-egui/src/dock.rs, revision 2a460986ee7a4b32656a3d434849693904b486bc.
  https://github.com/storytold/effectcraft
  Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the EffectCraft contributors.
The widget-state styling in crates/ui/src/theme is adapted from VectorCraft (above),
crates/ui-egui/src/theme.rs, and:
- DesignCraft, crates/ui-egui/src/theme.rs, revision 66c7ce7e96ec286a091878cc2541ee8369aa79d6.
  https://github.com/storytold/designcraft
  Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the DesignCraft contributors.
The transactional panel model and drop-zone semantics in crates/ui/src/docking draw on the
FilmCraft/EffectCraft split, tab and accordion layouts and VectorCraft floating groups listed
above. The generic transaction validation, optional renderer and workspace-location helpers
are implemented here without dependencies on application engines or native window helpers.
All sources listed above are licensed MIT OR Apache-2.0. No icon artwork is copied here.

The PDF spooler in crates/print is extracted from:
- PdfCraft, crates/print/src/spool.rs, windows_print.ps1 and spooler tests,
  revision 33c14e07cfd742a82bf03baa6b103c01008fa68c.
  https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft
  Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the PdfCraft contributors.
  Licensed MIT OR Apache-2.0. PDF composition and application code are not copied.

The Windows printer duplex-capability query and parser additionally draw on PdfCraft,
crates/print/src/spool.rs, revision fb97ce958dfe92b6762ce40615c7836551710139 (PR #1154), under the same licenses above.
